local function initialize()
    _G.SXE_JUMP=_G.SXE_JUMP or {mov=0,v3=0,inf=0}
    _G.SXE_TEL=_G.SXE_TEL or {n=0}
    _G.SXE_TEL_ROTAS=_G.SXE_TEL_ROTAS or {}
    _G.SXE_TELPUT=_G.SXE_TELPUT or function(fields) for key,value in pairs(fields) do _G.SXE_TEL[key]=value end end
repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()
_G.SXELimitarPos = function(frame, x, y)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize
    if not vp then return x, y end
    local sz = frame.AbsoluteSize
    return math.clamp(x, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - sz.X)),
           math.clamp(y, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - sz.Y))
end
_G.SXEEscrevePos = function(frame, x, y)
    local pai = frame.Parent
    local pa = (pai and pai:IsA("GuiObject")) and pai.AbsolutePosition or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    local an, sz = frame.AnchorPoint, frame.AbsoluteSize
    frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
        math.floor(x - pa.X + an.X * sz.X),
        math.floor(y - pa.Y + an.Y * sz.Y))
end
_G.SXEGuiRoot = function()
    return (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
end
_G.SXEBuscaGui = function(nome)
    local r
    pcall(function() r = _G.SXEGuiRoot():FindFirstChild(nome) end)
    if r then return r end
    pcall(function()
        local pg = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            :FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        r = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(nome)
    end)
    return r
end
do
    _G.__SXET0 = _G.__SXET0 or os.clock()

        print(("[LOAD %6.2fs] %s"):format(os.clock() - _G.__SXET0, tostring(msg)))
    end
    _G.__LMARK("hub subiu")
    task.delay(10, function()
        for i = 1, 2 do
            local ss = SharedState
            local c  = (type(ss) == "table") and ss.AllAnimalsCache or nil
            _G.__LMARK(("SENTINELA %d/2 -> def=%s ent=%s diag=%s erro=%s pets=%s")
                :format(i,
                    tostring(_G.SXE_TocarScan ~= nil),
                    tostring(_G.__SXEEntrouDito == true),
                    tostring(_G.__SXEDiagDito == true),
                    tostring(_G.__SXEScanErroDito == true),
                    tostring(c and #c or "sem SharedState")))
            if i == 1 then task.wait(6) end
        end
    end)
    _G.SXE_TEL       = _G.SXE_TEL or { n = 0 }
    _G.SXE_TEL_ROTAS = _G.SXE_TEL_ROTAS or {}
    _G.SXE_TELPUT    = _G.SXE_TELPUT or function(campos)
        local t = _G.SXE_TEL
        if type(t) ~= "table" or type(campos) ~= "table" then return end
        for k, v in pairs(campos) do t[k] = v end
        t.ultimo = os.clock()
    end

        j.mov or 0, j.v3 or 0, j.inf or 0))

end

    _G.SXE_JUMP = { mov = 0, v3 = 0, inf = 0 }
end

_G.SXE_GanchoDiag = "nao iniciado"
do
    local _remoto, _job
    local function regra(pasta)
        local filhos = pasta:GetChildren()
        if #filhos < 8 then
            return nil, ("pasta com so %d filhos -- cedo demais"):format(#filhos)
        end
        local re, rf = {}, {}
        for _, ch in ipairs(filhos) do
            local pfx, resto = tostring(ch.Name):match("^(R[EF])/(.+)$")
            if pfx and resto and #resto >= 32 and resto:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x") then
                if pfx == "RE" then re[resto] = ch else rf[resto] = ch end
            end
        end
        local ancora, pares = nil, 0
        for h, ch in pairs(re) do
            if rf[h] then pares = pares + 1; ancora = ch end
        end
        if pares ~= 1 then
            return nil, ("ancora ambigua: %d pares RE+RF"):format(pares)
        end
        local idx
        for i, ch in ipairs(filhos) do
            if ch == ancora then idx = i break end
        end
        if not idx or idx < 2 then return nil, "ancora sem vizinho anterior" end
        local alvo = filhos[idx - 1]
        if not alvo or not alvo:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            return nil, ("vizinho nao e RemoteEvent (%s)")
                :format(alvo and alvo.ClassName or "nil")
        end
        return alvo, ("ok: idx=%d ancora=%d de %d filhos"):format(idx - 1, idx, #filhos)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local job = game.JobId
        local pasta
        for tentativa = 1, 3 do
            pcall(function()
                local RSf = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local pk  = RSf:WaitForChild("Packages", 20)
                pasta = pk and pk:WaitForChild("Net", 15)
            end)
            if pasta then
                if tentativa > 1 then
                    print("[GANCHO] Packages.Net achado na tentativa " .. tentativa)
                end
                break
            end
            task.wait(2)
        end
        if not pasta then
            _G.SXE_GanchoDiag = "Packages.Net nao apareceu (3 tentativas)"
            print("[GANCHO] Packages.Net nao apareceu apos 3 tentativas")
            return
        end
        local t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            local r, diag = regra(pasta)
            _G.SXE_GanchoDiag = diag
            if r then
                _remoto, _job = r, job
                _G.SXE_GanchoDiag = diag .. "  ->  " .. tostring(r.Name)
                print("[GANCHO] RESOLVIDO: " .. tostring(r.Name))
                print("[GANCHO] " .. tostring(diag))
                return
            end
            task.wait(1)
        until os.clock() - t0 > 60
        print("[GANCHO] NAO RESOLVEU em 60s -- " .. tostring(_G.SXE_GanchoDiag))
    end)
    _G.SXE_GanchoRemote = function()
        if _remoto and _job == game.JobId then return _remoto end
        return nil
    end
end

local function deepMerge(dst, src)
    for k, v in pairs(src) do
        if type(v) == "table" and type(dst[k]) == "table" then deepMerge(dst[k], v) else dst[k] = v end
    end
end
local OwnedConfigKeys={["StealQuick"]=true,["QuickStealKey"]=true,["StealFireKey"]=true,["StealSettings"]=true,["AutoDepositar"]=true,["AutoGrabRadius"]=true,["AutoStealEnabled"]=true,["AutoTPCFrameFloor1"]=true,["AutoTPCFrameFloors"]=true,["AutoTPFloor2FromFloor1"]=true,["AutoTPHighestGen"]=true,["AutoTPHighestValue"]=true,["AutoTPPreFire"]=true,["AutoTPPriority"]=true,["ConfigKey"]=true,["DontGriefFMLY"]=true,["DontGriefSON"]=true,["InstantStealEnabled"]=true,["MenuKey"]=true,["MobileBtnPos"]=true,["MobileButton"]=true,["MobileMode"]=true,["MutationList"]=true,["MutationPriority"]=true,["NoRenderTP"]=true,["NoRenderTPSteal"]=true,["Positions"]=true,["PriorityList"]=true,["SXEPositions"]=true,["SoltarFlightHold"]=true,["StealHighest"]=true,["StealMode"]=true,["StealModeKey"]=true,["StealNearest"]=true,["StealPriority"]=true,["StealTargetUID"]=true,["TeleportKey"]=true,["TpSettings"]=true,["UILocked"]=true,["Visibilities"]=true,["sizes"]=true}
local Config = {}
deepMerge(Config, DefaultConfig)
local function readConfig(path)
    if not (readfile and isfile and isfile(path)) then return nil end
    local ok,data=pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)) end)
    return ok and type(data)=='table' and data or nil
end
local function importOwned(data,legacy)
    if not data then return end
    for key,value in pairs(data) do
        if OwnedConfigKeys[key] and not (legacy and ModuleName=='Teleport' and (key=='ConfigKey' or key=='MobileBtnPos')) then
            if type(value)=='table' and type(Config[key])=='table' then deepMerge(Config[key],value)
            else Config[key]=value end
        end
    end
end
local saved=readConfig(FileName)
importOwned(saved or readConfig('heresy_compact.json'),saved==nil)
local function SaveConfig()
    if not writefile then return end
    local own={}
    for key,value in pairs(Config) do if OwnedConfigKeys[key] then own[key]=value end end
    pcall(function() writefile(FileName,HttpService:JSONEncode(own)) end)
end

local function ShowNotification(title, text)
    local h = UI.__SXENotif
    if not (h and h.f and h.f.Parent) then
        local old = _G.SXEBuscaGui("HeresyTeleportNotification"); if old then old:Destroy() end
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui", _G.SXEGuiRoot()); sg.Name = "HeresyTeleportNotification"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false; sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true; sg.DisplayOrder = 1000000
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", sg); f.Size = UDim2.new(0,290,0,54); f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-145,0,118)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background; f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04; f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.Visible = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,12)
        local o = Instance.new("UIStroke", f); o.Name = "HeresyOutline"; o.Color = Theme.Accent
        o.Thickness = 1.4; o.Transparency = 0.1; o.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        local ac = Instance.new("Frame", f); ac.Name = "HeresyAccent"; ac.Size = UDim2.new(0,3,0,26)
        ac.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,14); ac.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent; ac.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local t1 = Instance.new("TextLabel", f); t1.Size = UDim2.new(1,-30,0,18); t1.Position = UDim2.new(0,20,0,8)
        t1.BackgroundTransparency = 1; t1.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; t1.TextSize = 11
        t1.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent1; t1.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        local t2 = Instance.new("TextLabel", f); t2.Size = UDim2.new(1,-30,0,15); t2.Position = UDim2.new(0,20,0,28)
        t2.BackgroundTransparency = 1; t2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; t2.TextSize = 10
        t2.TextColor3 = Theme.TextSecondary; t2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        h = {f = f, t1 = t1, t2 = t2, n = 0}
        UI.__SXENotif = h
    end
    h.t1.Text = tostring(title):upper()
    h.t2.Text = tostring(text)
    h.f.Visible = true
    h.n = h.n + 1
    local mine = h.n
    task.delay(2, function() if h.n == mine then h.f.Visible = false end end)
end
UI._SXESliderDragging = false
local function rememberPosition(name, frame)
    if not name or not frame then return end
    if not Config.SXEPositions then Config.SXEPositions = {} end
    local abs = frame.AbsolutePosition
    Config.SXEPositions[name] = {xs=0, xo=abs.X, ys=0, yo=abs.Y}
    SaveConfig()
end
local function applySavedPosition(name, frame)
    if not name or not frame then return end
    local x, y
    local d = Config.SXEPositions and Config.SXEPositions[name]
    if d then
        x, y = d.xo or 0, d.yo or 0
    else
        local p = Config.Positions and Config.Positions[name]
        if not p then return end
        local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        x = math.floor((p.X or 0) * vp.X)
        y = math.floor((p.Y or 0) * vp.Y)
    end
    task.defer(function()
        if not frame.Parent then return end
        local cx, cy = x, y
        if _G.SXELimitarPos then cx, cy = _G.SXELimitarPos(frame, x, y) end
        _G.SXEEscrevePos(frame, cx, cy)
    end)
end
UI.SXE_EhMobile = function()
    local m = tostring((Config and Config.MobileMode) or "auto"):lower()
    if m == "on" then return true end
    if m == "off" then return false end
    return UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled
end
local _paineisReg = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
UI.SXE_EncaixarPainel = function(f)
    if not f or not f.Parent then return end
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize
    if not vp or vp.X <= 0 or vp.Y <= 0 then return end
    local aw = tonumber(f.AbsoluteSize.X) or 0
    local ah = tonumber(f.AbsoluteSize.Y) or 0
    local px = tonumber(f.Position.X.Offset) or 0
    local py = tonumber(f.Position.Y.Offset) or 0
    if aw <= 0 or ah <= 0 then return end
    local tetoW, tetoH = vp.X - 16, vp.Y - 96
    if UI.SXE_EhMobile() then
        tetoW = math.min(tetoW, vp.X * 0.9)
        tetoH = math.min(tetoH, vp.Y * 0.55)
    end
    local w = math.max(150, math.min(aw, tetoW))
    local h = math.max(150, math.min(ah, tetoH))
    if w ~= aw or h ~= ah then f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h) end
    local x = math.clamp(px, 8, math.max(8, vp.X - w - 8))
    local y = math.clamp(py, 8, math.max(8, vp.Y - h - 8))
    if x ~= px or y ~= py then f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y) end
end
UI.SXE_EncaixarTodos = function()
    for f in pairs(_paineisReg) do pcall(UI.SXE_EncaixarPainel, f) end
end
UI.SXE_RegistrarPainel = function(f)
    if not f then return end
    _paineisReg[f] = true
    task.defer(function() pcall(UI.SXE_EncaixarPainel, f) end)
    task.delay(0.6, function() pcall(UI.SXE_EncaixarPainel, f) end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
            UI.SXE_EncaixarTodos()
        end)
    end)
end)

local _plotCtrl, _plotTable
local _jaEsperou = false
local function _moduloCarregado(inst)
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local f = env and env.getloadedmodules
    _G.__SXETemGLM = (type(f) == "function")
    if type(f) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local ok, lista = pcall(f)
    if not ok or type(lista) ~= "table" then return nil end
    for i = 1, #lista do
        if lista[i] == inst then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function _loadPlotTable()
    if type(_plotTable) == "table" then return _plotTable end
    local _esperou = 0
    pcall(function()
        local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local controllers = RS:WaitForChild("Controllers", 10)
        local mod = controllers:WaitForChild("PlotController", 10)
        if not mod then return end
        if not _jaEsperou then
            _jaEsperou = true
            local teto = tonumber(_G.SXE_EsperarModulo) or 3
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while _moduloCarregado(mod) == false and os.clock() - t0 < teto do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            _esperou = os.clock() - t0
        end
        _plotCtrl = require(mod)
        _plotTable = debug.getupvalue(_plotCtrl.GetPlots, 1)
        if type(_plotTable) ~= "table" and type(setthreadidentity) == "function" then
            local ant
            if type(getthreadidentity) == "function" then
                pcall(function() ant = getthreadidentity() end)
            end
            pcall(setthreadidentity, 8)
            pcall(function()
                _plotCtrl = require(mod)
                _plotTable = debug.getupvalue(_plotCtrl.GetPlots, 1)
            end)
            if ant then pcall(setthreadidentity, ant) end
        end
    end)
    _G.XenSyncDiag = type(_plotTable) == "table" and "PlotController upvalue" or "PlotController upvalue unavailable"
    if type(_plotTable) == "table" and not _G.__SXEPlotTableOK and _G.__LMARK then
        _G.__LMARK(("plot table pronta (esperei %.2fs, getloadedmodules=%s)")
            :format(_esperou, _G.__SXETemGLM and "sim" or "NAO"))
    end
    if type(_plotTable) == "table" then _G.__SXEPlotTableOK = true end
    return type(_plotTable) == "table" and _plotTable or nil
end
_G.XenSyncAll = _G.XenSyncAll or function()
    local plots = _loadPlotTable()
    local out = {}
    if type(plots) ~= "table" then return out end
    for key, plotClient in pairs(plots) do
        if type(plotClient) == "table" and plotClient.Channel then
            out[tostring(key)] = plotClient.Channel
            if typeof(plotClient.PlotModel) == "Instance" then
                out[plotClient.PlotModel.Name] = plotClient.Channel
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end
_G.XenSyncGet = _G.XenSyncGet or function(idx)
    local plots = _loadPlotTable()
    if type(plots) ~= "table" or idx == nil then return nil end
    local pc = plots[idx]
    if type(pc) == "table" then return pc.Channel end
    for key, plotClient in pairs(plots) do
        if tostring(key) == tostring(idx) then
            return type(plotClient) == "table" and plotClient.Channel or nil
        end
        if type(plotClient) == "table" and typeof(plotClient.PlotModel) == "Instance"
           and plotClient.PlotModel.Name == tostring(idx) then
            return plotClient.Channel
        end
    end
    return nil
end
_G.sProp = _G.sProp or function(ch, key)
    if type(ch) ~= "table" or key == nil then return nil end
    local ct = rawget(ch, "CacheTable")
    if type(ct) == "table" then return ct[key] end
    return nil
end
local _AD, _MD, _TD, _GD
local function _data()
    if _AD then return true end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local d = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Datas", 10)
        if not d then return end
        local mA = d:WaitForChild("Animals", 10)
        if not mA then return end
        _AD = require(mA)
        local mM = d:WaitForChild("Mutations", 10)
        if mM then _MD = require(mM) end
        local mT = d:WaitForChild("Traits", 10)
        if mT then _TD = require(mT) end
        pcall(function() local g = d:WaitForChild("Game", 10); if g then _GD = require(g) end end)
    end)
    return ok and _AD ~= nil
end
local _genMemo = {}
_G._xenGen = _G._xenGen or function(index, mutation, traits)
    if not _data() then return 0 end
    local info = _AD[index]
    if not info then return 0 end
    local chave = tostring(index) .. "|" .. tostring(mutation)
    if type(traits) == "table" then chave = chave .. "|" .. table.concat(traits, ",") end
    local memo = _genMemo[chave]
    if memo ~= nil then return memo end
    local base = info.Generation
    if not base then
        local mod = (_GD and _GD.Game and _GD.Game.AnimalGanerationModifier) or 0.1
        base = (info.Price or 0) * mod
    end
    local mult, sleepy = 1, false
    if type(traits) == "table" then
        for _, tr in ipairs(traits) do
            local t = _TD[tr]
            if t then
                if tr == "Sleepy" then sleepy = true
                elseif t.MultiplierModifier then mult = mult + t.MultiplierModifier end
            end
        end
    end
    local r = base * mult
    if sleepy then r = r * 0.5 end
    r = math.round(r)
    _genMemo[chave] = r
    return r
end
_G.SXE_PetResolve = _G.SXE_PetResolve or function(key)
    if key == nil or not _data() then return nil, 0 end
    key = tostring(key)
    local info = _AD[key]
    if not info then
        for idx, i in pairs(_AD) do
            if type(i) == "table" and i.DisplayName == key then
                info, key = i, idx; break
            end
        end
    end
    if type(info) ~= "table" then return nil, 0 end
    local gen = (_G._xenGen and _G._xenGen(key, nil, nil)) or 0
    return info.DisplayName or key, gen
end
_G.SXE_ChannelGet = _G.SXE_ChannelGet or _G.sProp
_G.SXE_GetPlotChannel = _G.SXE_GetPlotChannel or function(name) return _G.XenSyncGet(tostring(name)) end
_G.SXE_AllCachedChannels = _G.SXE_AllCachedChannels or function() return _G.XenSyncAll() or {} end
task.spawn(function()
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat
        plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then task.wait(0.05) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    local done, warmT0 = {}, os.clock()
    while (os.clock() - warmT0) < 15 do
        local kids = plots:GetChildren()
        local pending = false
        for _, plot in ipairs(kids) do
            if not done[plot.Name] then
                local ch = _G.XenSyncGet and _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name)
                if ch then
                    local al = _G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList")
                    if al ~= nil then done[plot.Name] = true else pending = true end
                else
                    pending = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not pending and #kids > 0 then break end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    if not Workspace.StreamingEnabled then return end
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots"); if not plots then task.wait(0.1) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    local function plotPos(plot)
        local ok, pv = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot().Position end)
        if ok and pv and pv.Magnitude > 1 then return pv end
        if plot.PrimaryPart then return plot.PrimaryPart.Position end
        local bp = plot:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return bp and bp.Position or nil
    end
    local pending = 0
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local pos = plotPos(plot)
        if pos then
            pending = pending + 1
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos) end)
                pending = pending - 1
            end)
        end
    end
    local sw = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - sw < 10 do task.wait(0.05) end
end)

local function makeOneWay(plat)
    if not plat then return end
    local rsConn
    local lastY = nil
    rsConn = game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not plat or not plat.Parent then
            if rsConn then rsConn:Disconnect() end
            return
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            local currentY = hrp.Position.Y
            if not lastY then lastY = currentY end
            local deltaY = currentY - lastY
            local isMovingUp = (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1) or (deltaY > 0.01 and deltaY < 5)
            if isMovingUp then
                plat.CanCollide = false
            else
                if currentY > plat.Position.Y + 0.1 then
                    plat.CanCollide = true
                else
                    plat.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
            lastY = currentY
        end
    end)
end

SharedState = {SelectedPetData=nil, AllAnimalsCache={}, InitialScanComplete=false}
_G.SXE_ScanRules = (function()
    local rules = {}
    local blockedMachines = {Fuse=true, Duel=true, Trade=true, Crafting=true}
    function rules.Unavailable(animal)
        if type(animal) ~= 'table' then return true end
        local machine = animal.Machine
        return type(machine) == 'table' and blockedMachines[machine.Type] == true
    end
    function rules.ResolveOwner(owner)
        local found
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(owner) == 'Instance' then
                if owner:IsA('Player') then found=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner.UserId)
                else found=Players:FindFirstChild(owner.Name) end
            elseif type(owner) == 'number' then
                found=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner)
            elseif type(owner) == 'table' then
                if tonumber(owner.UserId) then found=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(owner.UserId)) end
                if not found and owner.Name then found=rules.ResolveOwner(tostring(owner.Name)) end
            elseif type(owner) == 'string' then
                if tonumber(owner) then found=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(owner));return end
                local name=owner:lower()
                local displayMatch, matches=nil,0
                for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p.Name:lower()==name then found=p;return end
                    if p.DisplayName:lower()==name then displayMatch=p;matches+=1 end
                end
                if matches==1 then found=displayMatch end
            end
        end)
        return found
    end
    function rules.OwnerAllowed(owner)
        if owner == nil then return false end
        local resolved=rules.ResolveOwner(owner)
        if resolved then
            if resolved.UserId==LocalPlayer.UserId then return false end
            local excluded=_G.apBlacklist
            if type(excluded)=='table' and (excluded[resolved.UserId]==true or excluded[tostring(resolved.UserId)]==true) then return false end
            return true
        end
        if type(owner)=='string' then
            local name=owner:lower()
            if name==LocalPlayer.Name:lower() or name==LocalPlayer.DisplayName:lower() then return false end
            if tonumber(owner) then return false end
        end
        if typeof(owner)=='Instance' or type(owner)=='number'
            or (type(owner)=='table' and (owner.UserId or owner.Name)) then return false end
        return true
    end
    function rules.OwnerName(owner)
        local resolved=rules.ResolveOwner(owner)
        if resolved then return resolved.Name end
        if type(owner)=='string' then return owner end
        if type(owner)=='table' and owner.Name then return tostring(owner.Name) end
        return nil
    end
    return rules
end)()
do
    local cachedKey, priorityRanks, mutationRanks
    local function normalize(value)
        return tostring(value or ''):lower():gsub("[%s%-_'%.]", '')
    end
    local function lookups()
        local names=priorityList or {}
        local mutations=mutationList or Config.MutationList or {}
        local key=table.concat(names,'\0')..'\1'..table.concat(mutations,'\0')
        if key~=cachedKey then
            cachedKey=key;priorityRanks={};mutationRanks={}
            for i=#names,1,-1 do priorityRanks[normalize(names[i])]=i end
            for i=#mutations,1,-1 do mutationRanks[normalize(mutations[i])]=i end
        end
        return priorityRanks,mutationRanks
    end
    _G.SXE_PriorityRank=function(pet)
        local ranks=lookups()
        return ranks[normalize(pet.name or pet.petName)] or ranks[normalize(pet.index)]
    end
    _G.SXE_SortPets=function(pets,mode)
        local ranks,mutations=lookups()
        local anyPriority=false
        for _,pet in ipairs(pets) do
            pet._pri=ranks[normalize(pet.name or pet.petName)] or ranks[normalize(pet.index)]
            local mut=pet.mutation
            if not mut or mut=='' or mut=='None' then mut='Normal' end
            pet._mut=mutations[normalize(mut)] or mutations.normal or math.huge
            if pet._pri then anyPriority=true end
        end
        local highest=(mode or Config.StealMode)=='Highest' or not anyPriority
        table.sort(pets,function(a,b)
            if not highest then
                if (a._pri~=nil)~=(b._pri~=nil) then return a._pri~=nil end
                if a._pri and b._pri and a._pri~=b._pri then return a._pri<b._pri end
                if Config.MutationPriority~=false and a._mut~=b._mut then return a._mut<b._mut end
            end
            local av=tonumber(a.mps or a.genValue or a.mpsValue) or 0
            local bv=tonumber(b.mps or b.genValue or b.mpsValue) or 0
            if av~=bv then return av>bv end
            local au=a.uid or tostring(a.plot)..'_'..tostring(a.slot)
            local bu=b.uid or tostring(b.plot)..'_'..tostring(b.slot)
            return au<bu
        end)
        return pets
    end
end
_G.SXE_ReadRagdoll = function()
    -- Merkov2 Auto Steal: only the server's RagdollEndTime determines the wait.
    local endTime=tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute('RagdollEndTime'))
    if not endTime or endTime~=endTime or math.abs(endTime)==math.huge then
        return false,0,nil
    end
    local remaining=endTime-workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    if remaining>0 then return true,remaining,endTime end
    return false,0,endTime
end

task.spawn(function()
    _G.__SXEEntrouDito = true
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local AnimalsShared, AnimalsData, NumberUtils
    local channels, channelCount, animalChannelCount, animalRawCount = nil, 0, 0, 0
    local connectedChannels = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local scanBusy = false
    local modulesReady = false
    local loadingModules = false
    local lastModuleAttempt = 0
    local lastGcScan = 0
    local function loadModules()
        if modulesReady then return true end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared", 15)
            AnimalsShared = require(shared:WaitForChild("Animals", 15))
            AnimalsData = AnimalsData or _AD
            task.spawn(function()
                local d = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas", 10)
                local mA = d and d:WaitForChild("Animals", 10)
                if mA then pcall(function() AnimalsData = require(mA) or AnimalsData end) end
                local u = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Utils", 10)
                local mN = u and u:WaitForChild("NumberUtils", 10)
                if mN then pcall(function() NumberUtils = require(mN) end) end
            end)
            local expected = debug.getupvalue(AnimalsShared.GetGeneration, 2)
            _G.__SXEGenPatchOK = false
            if type(expected) == "string" then
                local _stub = function() return expected end
                debug.setupvalue(AnimalsShared.GetGeneration, 1, _stub)
                _G.__SXEGenPatchOK =
                    (debug.getupvalue(AnimalsShared.GetGeneration, 1) == _stub)
            end
        end)
        modulesReady = ok and type(AnimalsShared) == "table"
        return modulesReady
    end
    local function startLoadModules()
        if modulesReady or loadingModules or os.clock() - lastModuleAttempt < 1 then return end
        loadingModules = true
        lastModuleAttempt = os.clock()
        task.spawn(function()
            loadModules()
            loadingModules = false
        end)
    end
    local function getChannelMethod(channel, method)
        if type(channel) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local direct = rawget(channel, method)
        if type(direct) == "function" then return direct end
        local mt = getmetatable(channel)
        local index = type(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index")
        if type(index) == "table" then
            local value = rawget(index, method)
            if type(value) == "function" then return value end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function channelGet(channel, key)
        if type(channel) ~= "table" or key == nil then return nil end
        local cache = rawget(channel, "CacheTable")
        if type(cache) == "table" then return cache[key] end
        return nil
    end
    local function isSyncChannel(channel)
        return type(channel) == "table"
            and type(rawget(channel, "CacheTable")) == "table"
            and getChannelMethod(channel, "OnChanged") ~= nil
    end
    local function targetAnimalChannels()
        return math.clamp(#Players:GetPlayers(), 1, 8)
    end
    local function countChannels(value, limit)
        if type(value) ~= "table" then return 0 end
        local count = 0
        for _, channel in pairs(value) do
            if isSyncChannel(channel) then
                count += 1
                if limit and count >= limit then break end
            end
        end
        return count
    end
    local function countAnimalChannels(value, limit)
        if type(value) ~= "table" then return 0 end
        local count = 0
        for _, channel in pairs(value) do
            if isSyncChannel(channel) and type(channelGet(channel, "AnimalList")) == "table" then
                count += 1
                if limit and count >= limit then break end
            end
        end
        return count
    end
    local function countAnimalEntries(value, limit)
        if type(value) ~= "table" then return 0 end
        local count = 0
        for _, channel in pairs(value) do
            if isSyncChannel(channel) then
                local animalList = channelGet(channel, "AnimalList")
                if type(animalList) == "table" then
                    for _, brainrot in pairs(animalList) do
                        if type(brainrot) == "table" and (brainrot.Index or brainrot.Name or brainrot.Animal) then
                            count += 1
                            if limit and count >= limit then return count end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return count
    end
    local function _chavesDePlot()
        local nomes, n = {}, 0
        pcall(function()
            local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if not pl then return end
            for _, p in ipairs(pl:GetChildren()) do
                n = n + 1; nomes[n] = p.Name
                if n >= 3 then break end
            end
        end)
        local tentativas = {}
        if n >= 2 then tentativas[#tentativas + 1] = { nomes[1], nomes[2] } end
        if n >= 3 then tentativas[#tentativas + 1] = { nomes[2], nomes[3] } end
        return tentativas
    end
    local _mapaCache, _mapaT = nil, 0
    local function _canaisDoPlotTable()
        if not _G.__SXEPlotTableOK then return nil end
        if type(_G.XenSyncAll) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local agora = os.clock()
        if _mapaCache and (agora - _mapaT) < (tonumber(_G.SXE_ScanMapaGap) or 0.05) then
            return _mapaCache
        end
        local ok, mapa = pcall(_G.XenSyncAll)
        if not ok or type(mapa) ~= "table" or next(mapa) == nil then return nil end
        _mapaCache, _mapaT = mapa, agora
        return mapa
    end
    local function getSyncChannels()
        local best, bestCount, bestAnimalCount, bestRawCount = nil, 0, 0, 0
        local _direto = _canaisDoPlotTable()
        if _direto then
            local _an = countAnimalChannels(_direto, 8)
            if _an > 0 then
                _G.SXE_ScannerCamada = "plots"
                return _direto, countChannels(_direto, 16), _an,
                       countAnimalEntries(_direto, 64)
            end
        end
        local function _avaliar(value)
            if type(value) ~= "table" then return end
            local animals = countAnimalChannels(value, 8)
            local total = animals > 0 and countChannels(value, 16) or 0
            local raw = animals > 0 and countAnimalEntries(value, 64) or 0
            if raw > bestRawCount
                or (raw == bestRawCount and animals > bestAnimalCount)
                or (raw == bestRawCount and animals == bestAnimalCount and total > bestCount)
            then
                best, bestCount, bestAnimalCount, bestRawCount = value, total, animals, raw
            end
        end
        local _fgc = (getgenv and getgenv().filtergc)
        if type(_fgc) == "function" then
            for _, chaves in ipairs(_chavesDePlot()) do
                local ok, res = pcall(_fgc, "table", { Keys = chaves }, false)
                if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                    for _, t in ipairs(res) do _avaliar(t) end
                end
                if bestRawCount > 0 then break end
            end
            if best then
                _G.SXE_ScannerCamada = "filtergc"
                return best, bestCount, bestAnimalCount, bestRawCount
            end
        end
        if type(getgc) ~= "function" then
            _G.SXE_ScannerDiag = "sem filtergc e sem getgc"
            return nil, 0, 0, 0
        end
        _G.SXE_ScannerCamada = "getgc"
        for _, value in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
            if type(value) == "table" then
                local animals = countAnimalChannels(value, 8)
                local total = animals > 0 and countChannels(value, 16) or 0
                local raw = animals > 0 and countAnimalEntries(value, 64) or 0
                if raw > bestRawCount
                    or (raw == bestRawCount and animals > bestAnimalCount)
                    or (raw == bestRawCount and animals == bestAnimalCount and total > bestCount)
                then
                    best, bestCount, bestAnimalCount, bestRawCount = value, total, animals, raw
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestCount, bestAnimalCount, bestRawCount
    end
    local _promovido = false
    local function ensureChannels(force)
        local _naPlots = (_G.SXE_ScannerCamada == "plots")
        local _promover = false
        if not _naPlots and not _promovido and _G.__SXEPlotTableOK then
            _promovido, _promover = true, true
        end
        if not _naPlots and not _promover then
            if not force and channels and countAnimalChannels(channels, 1) > 0 then
                return true
            end
            if force and channels and os.clock() - lastGcScan < 1 then
                return true
            end
            if os.clock() - lastGcScan < 1 then
                return channels ~= nil
            end
        end
        lastGcScan = os.clock()
        channels, channelCount, animalChannelCount, animalRawCount = getSyncChannels()
        _G.SXE_ScannerDiag = ("%s %d total %d animal %d raw"):format(
            tostring(_G.SXE_ScannerCamada or "?"),
            channelCount or 0,
            animalChannelCount or 0,
            animalRawCount or 0
        )
        return channels ~= nil
    end
    local function allCachedChannels()
        ensureChannels(false)
        local out = {}
        for key, channel in pairs(channels or {}) do
            if isSyncChannel(channel) then
                out[tostring(key)] = channel
                local index = rawget(channel, "Index")
                if index ~= nil then out[tostring(index)] = channel end
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    _G.SXE_ChannelGet = channelGet
    _G.SXE_AllCachedChannels = allCachedChannels
    _G.SXE_GetPlotChannel = function(plotName)
        ensureChannels(false)
        plotName = tostring(plotName)
        for key, channel in pairs(channels or {}) do
            if isSyncChannel(channel) and (tostring(key) == plotName or tostring(rawget(channel, "Index")) == plotName) then
                return channel
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function traitArray(t)
        local out = {}
        if type(t) == "table" then
            for _, value in pairs(t) do
                local s = tostring(value)
                if s ~= "" and s ~= "None" then out[#out + 1] = s end
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    local _fmtMemo = {}
    local function fmtGen(v)
        v = tonumber(v) or 0
        local _fm = _fmtMemo[v]
        if _fm ~= nil then return _fm end
        if NumberUtils then
            local ok, s = pcall(function()
                return "$" .. NumberUtils:ToString(v) .. "/s"
            end)
            if ok then _fmtMemo[v] = s; return s end
        end
        if v >= 1e12 then return string.format("$%.2fT/s", v / 1e12)
        elseif v >= 1e9 then return string.format("$%.2fB/s", v / 1e9)
        elseif v >= 1e6 then return string.format("$%.2fM/s", v / 1e6)
        elseif v >= 1e3 then return string.format("$%.1fK/s", v / 1e3)
        else return string.format("$%.0f/s", v) end
    end
    _G.SXE_fmtGen = fmtGen
    local function ownerName(channel)
        return _G.SXE_ScanRules.OwnerName(channelGet(channel,'Owner'))
    end
    local _genCache = {}
    local function generationFor(index, mutation, traits)
        local _chave = tostring(index) .. "|" .. tostring(mutation)
        if type(traits) == "table" then
            local _ts = {}
            for _, v in pairs(traits) do _ts[#_ts + 1] = tostring(v) end
            table.sort(_ts)
            _chave = _chave .. "|" .. table.concat(_ts, ",")
        end
        local _memo = _genCache[_chave]
        if _memo ~= nil then return _memo end
        local generation = 0
        if _G.__SXEGenPatchOK and AnimalsShared then
            pcall(function()
                generation = AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(index, mutation, traits, nil) or 0
            end)
        end
        if type(generation) ~= "number" or generation <= 0 then
            generation = 0
            if _G._xenGen then
                pcall(function()
                    generation = _G._xenGen(index, mutation, traits) or 0
                end)
            end
        end
        local info = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[index]
        if (type(generation) ~= "number" or generation <= 0) and type(info) == "table" then
            generation = tonumber(info.Generation) or 0
        end
        local _r = type(generation) == "number" and generation or 0
        if _r > 0 then _genCache[_chave] = _r end
        return _r
    end
    local function _ordenaPets(a, b)
        if (a.genValue or 0) ~= (b.genValue or 0) then
            return (a.genValue or 0) > (b.genValue or 0)
        end
        return tostring(a.uid) < tostring(b.uid)
    end
    local function scanNow(force)
        if not modulesReady then startLoadModules();return 0 end
        if not ensureChannels(false) then return 0 end
        local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild('Plots')
        if not plots then return 0 end
        local children=plots:GetChildren()
        local cache,seen={},{}
        local plotsRead,channelsRead=0,0
        for _,plot in ipairs(children) do
            local channel=_G.SXE_GetPlotChannel(plot.Name)
            if not channel then
                local order=plot:GetAttribute('Order')
                if order~=nil then channel=_G.SXE_GetPlotChannel('Plot'..tostring(order)) end
            end
            if not channel then continue end
            channelsRead+=1
            local owner=channelGet(channel,'Owner')
            local list=channelGet(channel,'AnimalList')
            if type(list)~='table' then
                if owner==nil then plotsRead+=1 end
                continue
            end
            plotsRead+=1
            if seen[channel] then continue end
            seen[channel]=true
            if not _G.SXE_ScanRules.OwnerAllowed(owner) then continue end
            local ownerText=_G.SXE_ScanRules.OwnerName(owner) or '?'
            for slot,animal in pairs(list) do
                if _G.SXE_ScanRules.Unavailable(animal) then continue end
                local index=animal.Index or animal.Name or animal.Animal
                if index==nil then continue end
                local info=AnimalsData and AnimalsData[index]
                local name=(info and info.DisplayName) or tostring(index)
                local generation=generationFor(index,animal.Mutation,animal.Traits)
                local generationText=fmtGen(generation)
                local traits=traitArray(animal.Traits)
                cache[#cache+1]={name=name,petName=name,index=tostring(index),
                    genText=generationText,mpsText=generationText,genValue=generation,
                    mpsValue=generation,mps=generation,mutation=animal.Mutation or 'None',
                    traits=#traits>0 and table.concat(traits,', ') or 'None',owner=ownerText,
                    plot=plot.Name,slot=tostring(slot),uid=plot.Name..'_'..tostring(slot)}
            end
        end
        _G.SXE_SortPets(cache)
        SharedState.AllAnimalsCache=cache
        SharedState.ScanTimestamp=os.clock()
        SharedState.PlotsLidos=plotsRead
        SharedState.PlotsAlvo=#children
        SharedState.ChannelsReady=#children>0 and channelsRead==#children
        SharedState.InitialScanComplete=SharedState.ChannelsReady and plotsRead==#children
        return #cache
    end
    local function scanSafe(force)
        if scanBusy then return 0 end
        scanBusy = true
        local ok, result = pcall(scanNow, force)
        scanBusy = false
        if not ok and not _G.__SXEScanErroDito then
            _G.__SXEScanErroDito = true
            warn("[SXE] scan sync ESTOUROU: " .. tostring(result))
            if _G.__LMARK then _G.__LMARK("scan sync ESTOUROU: " .. tostring(result)) end
        end
        return ok and (tonumber(result) or 0) or 0
    end
    _G.SXE_TocarScan = scanSafe
    local function connectChannel(channel)
        if connectedChannels[channel] or not isSyncChannel(channel) then return false end
        local onChanged = getChannelMethod(channel, "OnChanged")
        if not onChanged then return false end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            onChanged(channel, "AnimalList", function()
                scanSafe()
            end)
            pcall(function() onChanged(channel, "Owner", function() scanSafe() end) end)
            local onIns = getChannelMethod(channel, "OnDictionaryInserted")
            if onIns then onIns(channel, "AnimalList", function() scanSafe() end) end
            local onRem = getChannelMethod(channel, "OnDictionaryRemoved")
            if onRem then onRem(channel, "AnimalList", function() scanSafe() end) end
        end)
        if ok then
            connectedChannels[channel] = true
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local function connectAllChannels()
        ensureChannels(false)
        local connected = 0
        for _, channel in pairs(channels or {}) do
            if connectChannel(channel) then connected += 1 end
        end
        return connected
    end
    local function refreshChannels()
        task.delay(0.5, function()
            ensureChannels(true)
            scanSafe()
            connectAllChannels()
        end)
    end
    startLoadModules()
    ensureChannels(false)
    scanSafe()
    connectAllChannels()
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(refreshChannels)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(refreshChannels)
    local bootStarted=os.clock()
    while true do
        startLoadModules()
        ensureChannels(false)
        scanSafe()
        connectAllChannels()
        task.wait(os.clock()-bootStarted<12 and 0.15 or (tonumber(_G.SXE_ScanPeriodo) or 2))
    end
end)
if not PlayerGui then PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10) end
selectedTargetUID = nil
manuallySelectedUID = Config.StealTargetUID
if manuallySelectedUID then selectedTargetUID = manuallySelectedUID end
local function saveStealTarget(uid)
    Config.StealTargetUID = uid
    manuallySelectedUID = uid
    selectedTargetUID = uid
    SaveConfig()
end
local function clearStealTarget()
    Config.StealTargetUID = nil
    manuallySelectedUID = nil
    selectedTargetUID = nil
    SharedState.SelectedPetData = nil
    SaveConfig()
end
function setStealMode(mode)
    Config.StealMode = mode
    Config.StealHighest = (mode == "Highest")
    Config.StealPriority = (mode == "Priority")
    Config.StealNearest = (mode == "Nearest")
    SaveConfig()
end
function get_all_pets()
    local out = {}
    local _mg = Config.StealNearest and Config.TpSettings.MinGenForGrab or nil
    if not _mg or _mg == "" then _mg = Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp end
    local minGen = (_mg and _mg ~= "" and parseMinGen(_mg)) or 0
    local _prio = {}
    if minGen > 0 then
        for _, nm in ipairs(priorityList) do _prio[tostring(nm):lower()] = true end
    end
    for _, a in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache or {}) do
        if a.plot and a.slot then
            local mps = a.genValue or a.mpsValue or a.mps or 0
            local _passa = (tonumber(mps) or 0) >= 1
            if _passa and minGen > 0 and (tonumber(mps) or 0) < minGen then
                local _nome = tostring(a.name or a.index or ""):lower()
                _passa = ((tonumber(mps) or 0) >= 10000000) or _prio[_nome] == true
            end
            if _passa then
            out[#out + 1] = {
                uid = a.plot .. "_" .. tostring(a.slot),
                petName = a.name or a.index,
                name = a.name or a.index,
                index = a.index,
                mpsValue = mps,
                gen = mps,
                genValue = mps,
                genText = a.genText,
                mutation = a.mutation,
                traits = a.traits,
                owner = a.owner,
                plot = a.plot,
                slot = a.slot,
                animalData = { plot = a.plot, slot = a.slot, name = a.name or a.index },
            }
            end
        end
    end
    _G.SXE_SortPets(out)
    return out
end
local _adorneeCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode="v"})
local _adorneeCacheAt = {}
function findAdorneeGlobal(animalData)
    if not animalData then return nil end
    local _ck = tostring(animalData.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(animalData.slot)
    local _c = _adorneeCache[_ck]
    if _c and _c.Parent and (os.clock() - (_adorneeCacheAt[_ck] or 0)) < 2 then return _c end
    local plot = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot)
    if plot then
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if podiums then
            local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot)
            if podium then
                local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                if base then
                    local spawn = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if spawn then _adorneeCache[_ck]=spawn; _adorneeCacheAt[_ck]=os.clock(); return spawn end
                    local _r = base:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or base
                    _adorneeCache[_ck]=_r; _adorneeCacheAt[_ck]=os.clock(); return _r
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
corner = function(o,r) local c=Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,r); c.Parent=o; return c end
tw = function(o,p,t) TweenService:Create(o,TweenInfo.new(t or 0.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),p):Play() end
HX = {}
addOutline = function(f) local o=Instance.new("UIStroke"); o.Name="HeresyOutline"; o.Color=Theme.Accent; o.Thickness=1.4; o.Transparency=0.1; o.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; o.Parent=f; return o end
function HX.btn(parent, text, size, cb)

    b.BackgroundTransparency=0; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.BorderSizePixel=0
    b.Text=text; b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize=12; b.TextColor3=Theme.Text

    st.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; st.Transparency=0.15; st.Parent=b

    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(st,{Transparency=0.15},0.12); st.Color=Theme.Stroke end)
    if cb then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb) end
    return b
end
